Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The informatics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 725 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 826 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to informatics theory, we decompose the problem into 725 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 826. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the informatics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 561 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 476. QED.
